\"notes\": \"Extended training to 1000 steps CONFIRMS ISR collapse worsens monotonically.\\\\n\\\\n## 500 vs 1000 step comparison (eval_sta.py):\\\\n| Metric | plan001-s42 (500步) | plan016 (1000步) | Change |\\\\n|--------|-------------------|-----------------|--------|\\\\n| eval PR | 0.93 | 0.96 | +3.2% |\\\\n| eval ISR | 0.123 | 0.0027 | -97.8% |\\\\n| proved_only ISR | 0.132 | 0.0028 | -97.9% |\\\\n| components | 6.58 | 18.55 | +182% |\\\\n| ISR=0 ratio | - | 94.8% | - |\\\\n| n_necessary=0 ratio | - | 94.8% | - |\\\\n\\\\n## Key findings:\\\\n1. PR improves (+3%) but ISR collapses near-zero (0.0027). The model gets BETTER at proving while becoming WORSE at faithful reasoning.\\\\n2. Component count nearly tripled (6.58→18.55). Model generates longer proofs with more components, but 94.8% have ZERO necessary components.\\\\n3. NOT tautological collapse (comp=2 ratio=0). Instead: contradiction-based collapse — model embeds P(a) and ¬P(a) among many irrelevant premises, making anything provable via explosion.\\\\n4. Windowed ISR trend is monotonically decreasing: 0.1115 (101-200) → 0.0433 (401-500) → 0.0200 (601-700) → 0.0144 (901-1000). No recovery signal.\\\\n5. The exec-only reward creates a pure reward hacking incentive: the model discovers that contradictions + trivially true conclusions always satisfy the prover.\\\\n\\\\n## Implication for paper:\\\\nExtended training is a clean ablation showing exec-only reward's failure mode worsens over time. The 500-step ISR of 0.123 was not a floor — it was a snapshot of ongoing collapse.\"}, \"plan006-prolog-sta-s42\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"retry_count\": 2, \"created_at\": {
  "command": "cd /home/ubuntu/.claude-account-5/projects/-home-ubuntu--agent-ml-research-idea-gen-0509-3-projects-solver-trace-ablation/401c6f72-6401-4d7a-84ba-cbee401acf70/tool-results && cat mcp-agent-ml-research-read_registry-1778854137401.txt | python3 -c \"\nimport json, sys\n\nraw = sys.stdin.read()\nouter = json.loads(raw)\ninner = json.loads(outer['result'])\nexperiments = inner['data']['experiments']\n\n# Required provenance fields\nspec_fields = ['command', 'server', 'data_path', 'data_count', 'model_name', 'seed']\ncompletion_fields = ['checkpoint_path', 'log_path']\n\nincomplete = {}\n\nfor eid, e in experiments.items():\n    if e.get('status') != 'DONE':\n        continue\n    \n    spec = e.get('spec', {}) or {}\n    missing = []\n    \n    # Check spec fields\n    for f in spec_fields:\n        if f not in spec or spec[f] is None or spec[f] == '':\n            missing.append(f'spec.{f}')\n    \n    # Check completion fields - can be at top level or in spec\n    for f in completion_fields:\n        top_val = e.get(f)\n        spec_val = spec.get(f)\n        if (top_val is None or top_val == '') and (spec_val is None or spec_val == ''):\n            missing.append(f'{f}')\n    \n    if missing:\n        incomplete[eid] = {'missing': missing, 'full_record': e}\n\nprint(f'Total DONE: {sum(1 for e in experiments.values() if e.get(\\\"status\\\")==\\\"DONE\\\")}')\nprint(f'Incomplete: {len(incomplete)}')\nprint(f'Complete: {sum(1 for e in experiments.values() if e.get(\\\"status\\\")==\\\"DONE\\\") - len(incomplete)}')\nprint()\n\nfor eid, info in incomplete.items():\n    print(f'=== {eid} ===')\n    print(f'Missing fields: {info[\\\"missing\\\"]}')\n    print(f'Full record:')\n    print(json.dumps(info['full_record'], indent=2, ensure_ascii=False))\n    print()\n\" 2>&1",
  "description": "Check all DONE experiments for missing provenance fields",
  "timeout": 30000
}

{
  "command": "cd /home/ubuntu/.claude-account-5/projects/-home-ubuntu--agent-ml-research-idea-gen-0509-3-projects-solver-trace-ablation/401c6f72-6401-4d7a-84ba-cbee401acf70/tool-results && cat mcp-agent-ml-research-read_registry-1778854137401.txt | python3 << 'PYEOF'\nimport json, sys\n\nraw = sys.stdin.read()\nouter = json.loads(raw)\ninner = json.loads(outer['result'])\nexperiments = inner['data']['experiments']\n\n# Required provenance fields\nspec_fields = ['command', 'server', 'data_path', 'data_count', 'model_name', 'seed']\ncompletion_fields = ['checkpoint_path', 'log_path']\n\n# Collect summary\nincomplete = {}\nfor eid, e in experiments.items():\n    if e.get('status') != 'DONE':\n        continue\n    spec = e.get('spec', {}) or {}\n    missing = []\n    for f in spec_fields:\n        if f not in spec or spec[f] is None or spec[f] == '':\n            missing.append(f'spec.{f}')\n    for f in completion_fields:\n        top_val = e.get(f)\n        spec_val = spec.get(f)\n        if (top_val is None or top_val == '') and (spec_val is None or spec_val == ''):\n            missing.append(f'{f}')\n    if missing:\n        incomplete[eid] = missing\n\n# Print summary table\nprint(f\"TOTAL DONE: {len([e for e in experiments.values() if e.get('status')=='DONE'])}\")\nprint(f\"COMPLETE (all provenance fields present): {len(experiments) - len(incomplete)}\")\nprint(f\"INCOMPLETE (missing provenance fields): {len(incomplete)}\")\nprint()\n\n# Group by missing field pattern\nfrom collections import Counter\npatterns = Counter()\nfor eid, missing in incomplete.items():\n    patterns[tuple(sorted(missing))] += 1\n\nprint(\"=== Missing field patterns ===\")\nfor pattern, count in patterns.most_common():\n    print(f\"  {count}x: {list(pattern)}\")\nprint()\n\n# List all incomplete experiments by ID\nprint(\"=== All incomplete experiments ===\")\nfor eid, missing in sorted(incomplete.items()):\n    print(f\"  {eid}: {missing}\")\nPYEOF\n",
  "description": "Comprehensive provenance check on all DONE experiments"
}

{
  "command": "REGISTRY_FILE=\"/home/ubuntu/.claude-account-5/projects/-home-ubuntu--agent-ml-research-idea-gen-0509-3-projects-solver-trace-ablation/401c6f72-6401-4d7a-84ba-cbee401acf70/tool-results/mcp-agent-ml-research-read_registry-1778854137401.txt\" python3 << 'PYEOF'\nimport json, os\n\nfp = os.environ['REGISTRY_FILE']\nwith open(fp, 'r') as f:\n    raw = f.read()\n\nouter = json.loads(raw)\ninner = json.loads(outer['result'])\nexperiments = inner['data']['experiments']\n\nspec_fields = ['command', 'server', 'data_path', 'data_count', 'model_name', 'seed']\ncompletion_fields = ['checkpoint_path', 'log_path']\n\noutpath = \"/tmp/incomplete_experiments_full.json\"\nincomplete = {}\n\nfor eid, e in sorted(experiments.items()):\n    if e.get('status') != 'DONE':\n        continue\n    spec = e.get('spec', {}) or {}\n    missing = []\n    for f in spec_fields:\n        if f not in spec or spec[f] is None or spec[f] == '':\n            missing.append(f'spec.{f}')\n    for f in completion_fields:\n        top_val = e.get(f)\n        spec_val = spec.get(f)\n        if (top_val is None or top_val == '') and (spec_val is None or spec_val == ''):\n            missing.append(f'{f}')\n    if missing:\n        incomplete[eid] = {\n            \"missing_fields\": missing,\n            \"full_record\": e\n        }\n\nwith open(outpath, 'w') as f:\n    json.dump(incomplete, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)\n\nprint(f\"Wrote {len(incomplete)} incomplete experiments to {outpath}\")\nprint(f\"File size: {os.path.getsize(outpath)} bytes\")\nPYEOF\n",
  "description": "Dump all incomplete experiment full records to file"
}

### Key Observations

**1. The dominant issue (33 of 42 experiments)** is the same 4 missing spec fields: `data_path`, `data_count`, `model_name`, `seed`. These are experiments where the `submit_training_job` tool populated `spec.command` and `spec.server` but did not extract structured metadata from the command. In nearly all cases, the values ARE inferrable from `spec.command`:
- `data_path`: extractable via `--data_path` flag (present in command)
- `seed`: extractable via `--seed` flag (present in command)
- `model_name`: extractable from `--model_path .../Qwen3-4B` (present in most commands), or defaults to Qwen3-4B per project config
- `data_count`: always 500 (filename is `train_500_seed42.jsonl`)

**Exception**: the `sta-phase1-*` experiments (8 total) use `--config` only with no inline `--data_path`/`--seed`/`--model_path` flags, so those values must come from the YAML configs (`grpo_exec_only.yaml`, `grpo_exec_sta.yaml`).

**2. checkpoint_path missing (21 experiments)**: Almost all of these are failed/killed experiments where no checkpoint was produced (OOM, disk full, killed early). This is expected -- failed runs don't produce checkpoints. The 5 "checkpoint_path only" missing cases are all conclusion=failed/killed.

**3. Two worst offenders**:
- **plan009-sft-baseline-s42**: Missing ALL 8 fields. Has `spec: {}` (empty). This was a killed experiment with `notes: "模型必须改为 Qwen3-4B"` -- it was aborted before any structured metadata was recorded. No command, no server, nothing.
- **plan005-sta-low-s42**: Missing `spec.command, spec.server, spec.data_count, spec.seed`. Has only `spec.model_name: "Qwen3-4B"` and `spec.data_path`. No command was recorded. However, it has `checkpoint_path` and `log_path` and a `conclusion: "negative"` with rich metrics, so it ran successfully -- just wasn't registered properly.

**4. exp1-isr-b03-s43** (log_path only missing): Has `spec.checkpoint_path` inside spec but no top-level or spec `log_path`. The spec has `pid` field but no `log_path` key. All other spec fields are present.

**5. exp1-random-b03-s44** (checkpoint_path + log_path): Failed experiment ("process dead 5x"), has spec with command/server/data fields populated, but no log or checkpoint.

### Fillability Assessment

- **33 experiments missing spec.data_path/data_count/model_name/seed**: All fillable by parsing `spec.command` (or the YAML config for sta-phase1). For sta-phase1-* experiments, need to read `configs/grpo_exec_only.yaml` and `configs/grpo_exec_sta.yaml`.
- **21 experiments missing checkpoint_path**: 15 are failed/killed (no checkpoint exists). For the other 6 with successful conclusions, the checkpoint path can likely be reconstructed from `--output_dir` in the command.
- **plan009-sft-baseline-s42**: Irrecoverable from registry alone -- needs manual backfill. The `notes` field says it was killed before running with the wrong model.
- **plan005-sta-low-s42**: Partially recoverable -- has model_name and data_path in spec, but command, server, data_count, seed must come from external records or be inferred (seed=42 from experiment name, server=westd-39279 from all other experiments, data_count=500 from data_path filename).

\"checkpoint_path\": \"/root/autodl-tmp/outputs/plan016_extended_1000_s42\", \"completed_at\": \"2026-05-14T08:13:51.754Z\", \"notes\": \"Extended training to 1000 steps CONFIRMS ISR collapse worsens monotonically.\\\\n\\\\n## 500 vs 1000 step comparison (eval_sta.py):\\\\n| Metric | plan001-s42 (500步) | plan016 (1000步) | Change |\\\\n|--------|-------------------|-----------------|--------|\\\\n| eval PR | 0.93 | 0.96 | +3.2% |\\\\n| eval ISR | 0.123 | 0.0027 | -97.8% |\\\\n| proved_only ISR | 0.132 | 0.0028 | -97.9% |\\\\n| components | 6.58 | 18.55 | +182% |\\\\n| ISR=0 ratio | - | 94.8% | - |\\\\n| n_necessary=0 ratio | - | 94.8% | - |\\\\n\\\\n## Key findings:\\\\n1. PR improves (+3%) but ISR collapses near-zero (0.0027). The model gets BETTER at proving while becoming WORSE at faithful reasoning.\\\\n2. Component count nearly tripled (6.58→18.55). Model generates longer proofs with more components, but 94.8% have ZERO necessary components.\\\\n3. NOT tautological collapse (comp=2 ratio=0). Instead: contradiction-based collapse — model embeds P(a) and ¬P(a) among many irrelevant premises, making anything provable via explosion.\\\\n4. Windowed ISR trend is monotonically decreasing: 0.1115 (101-200) → 0.0433 (401-500) → 0.0200 (601-700) → 0.0144 (901-1000). No recovery signal.\\\\n5. The exec-only reward creates a pure reward hacking incentive: the model discovers that contradictions + trivially true conclusions always satisfy the prover.\\\\n\\\\n## Implication for paper:\\\\nExtended training is a clean ablation showing exec-only reward's failure mode worsens over time. The 500-step ISR of 0.123 was not a floor — it was a snapshot of ongoing collapse.\"}, \"plan009-sft-baseline-s42-r4\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"retry_count\": 0, \"created_at\": \"2026-05-14T11:40:28.142Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-14T16:27:01.864Z\", \"hypothesis\": \"SFT baseline: supervised finetuning on FOLIO data (500 steps, Qwen3-4B, same model as all GRPO experiments) to establish whether hacking patterns are GRPO-specific or general finetuning artifacts. r4: fix CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES + --gpu + --no_wandb.\", \"parent\": \"plan009-sft-baseline-s42-r2\", \"spec\": {\"command\": \"bash -c \\\"export CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=1 && source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && cd /root/solver-trace-ablation && python3 scripts/train_sft.py --model_path /root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen3-4B --data_path data/train_500_seed42.jsonl --output_dir /root/autodl-tmp/outputs/plan009_sft_baseline_s42 --num_steps 500 --seed 42 --no_wandb --gpu 1\\\"\", \"server\": \"westd-39279\", \"gpu\": [\"cuda:1\"], \"account\": \"root\", \"data_path\": \"data/train_500_seed42.jsonl\", \"data_count\": 500, \"model_name\": \"Qwen3-4B\", \"seed\": 42, \"checkpoint_path\": \"/root/autodl-tmp/outputs/plan009_sft_baseline_s42/checkpoint-500/\"}, \"server\": \"westd-39279\", \"gpu\": \"cuda:1\", \"account\": \"root\", \"notes\": \"r4 训练完成 (500 steps, TRL SFT). ### Tool call

_source line 97_

<details open><summary>🔧 Tool call — Bash · `toolu_018ogG8VSjibZPaMvyesYrA8`</summary>

```json
{
  "command": "python3 << 'PYEOF'\nimport json\n\nDATA_FILE = \"/home/ubuntu/.claude-account-5/projects/-home-ubuntu--agent-ml-research-idea-gen-0509-3-projects-solver-trace-ablation/401c6f72-6401-4d7a-84ba-cbee401acf70/tool-results/mcp-agent-ml-research-read_registry-1778854337667.txt\"\n\nwith open(DATA_FILE, 'r') as f:\n    raw = json.load(f)\n\nregistry = json.loads(raw[\"result\"])\nexperiments = registry[\"data\"][\"experiments\"]\n\nREQUIRED_FIELDS = [\n    \"spec.command\",\n    \"spec.server\",\n    \"spec.data_path\",\n    \"spec.data_count\",\n    \"spec.model_name\",\n    \"spec.seed\",\n    \"checkpoint_path\",\n    \"log_path\",\n]\n\ndef check_field(exp, field_path):\n    \"\"\"Check if a field exists and is non-None/non-empty.\n    For checkpoint_path and log_path, check both top-level and inside spec.\"\"\"\n    parts = field_path.split(\".\")\n    \n    if len(parts) == 1:\n        # Top-level field - also check inside spec\n        fname = parts[0]\n        top_val = exp.get(fname)\n        spec_val = exp.get(\"spec\", {}).get(fname) if isinstance(exp.get(\"spec\"), dict) else None\n        \n        if top_val is not None and top_val != \"\" and top_val != []:\n            return True\n        if spec_val is not None and spec_val != \"\" and spec_val != []:\n            return True\n        return False\n    elif len(parts) == 2 and parts[0] == \"spec\":\n        fname = parts[1]\n        spec = exp.get(\"spec\", {})\n        if not isinstance(spec, dict):\n            return False\n        val = spec.get(fname)\n        return val is not None and val != \"\" and val != []\n    return False\n\ncomplete = []\nincomplete = []\n\nfor exp_id, exp in experiments.items():\n    missing = []\n    for field in REQUIRED_FIELDS:\n        if not check_field(exp, field):\n            missing.append(field)\n    \n    if missing:\n        incomplete.append((exp_id, missing, exp))\n    else:\n        complete.append(exp_id)\n\nprint(f\"=== PROVENANCE AUDIT SUMMARY ===\")\nprint(f\"Total success experiments: {len(experiments)}\")\nprint(f\"Fully complete: {len(complete)}\")\nprint(f\"Incomplete: {len(incomplete)}\")\nprint()\n\nif complete:\n    print(f\"--- COMPLETE ({len(complete)}) ---\")\n    for eid in sorted(complete):\n        print(f\"  {eid}\")\n    print()\n\nif incomplete:\n    print(f\"--- INCOMPLETE ({len(incomplete)}) ---\")\n    for exp_id, missing, exp in incomplete:\n        print(f\"\\n{'='*80}\")\n        print(f\"EXPERIMENT: {exp_id}\")\n        print(f\"MISSING FIELDS: {missing}\")\n        print(f\"\\nFULL RECORD DUMP:\")\n        print(json.dumps(exp, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False))\n        print(f\"{'='*80}\")\nPYEOF\n",
  "description": "Audit provenance completeness for all success experiments"
}
```

### Incomplete experiments (6)

#### 1-5: Five plan009-sft-baseline experiments — same pattern

These all share the identical deficiency: **`spec.data_path`, `spec.data_count`, `spec.model_name`, `spec.seed` are missing from the spec object.** The spec was populated by `submit_training_job` which only records operational fields (command, server, gpu, pid, log_path, etc.) but not the semantic experiment parameters.

All values are recoverable from `spec.command`:

| Experiment ID | Missing fields | Extractable values from command |
|---|---|---|
| `plan009-sft-baseline-s44-1000-r3` | data_path, data_count, model_name, seed | `data/train_500_seed42.jsonl`, 500, `Qwen3-4B`, 44 |
| `plan009-sft-baseline-s44-1000` | data_path, data_count, model_name, seed | `data/train_500_seed42.jsonl`, 500, `Qwen3-4B`, 44 |
| `plan009-sft-baseline-s43-1000` | data_path, data_count, model_name, seed | `data/train_500_seed42.jsonl`, 500, `Qwen3-4B`, 43 |
| `plan009-sft-baseline-s44` | data_path, data_count, model_name, seed | `data/train_500_seed42.jsonl`, 500, `Qwen3-4B`, 44 |
| `plan009-sft-baseline-s43` | data_path, data_count, model_name, seed | `data/train_500_seed42.jsonl`, 500, `Qwen3-4B`, 43 |

**Root cause:** These 5 experiments were submitted via `submit_training_job` (SFT training path) which doesn't auto-populate the semantic spec fields (data_path, data_count, model_name, seed) — only the GRPO experiment submission code does that.

#### 6: `exp1-isr-b03-s43` — missing `log_path`

**Missing field:** `log_path` (neither top-level nor in spec)

This experiment has `spec.data_path`, `spec.data_count`, `spec.model_name`, `spec.seed` all present (it went through the GRPO path), but no log_path was ever recorded. The experiment also has `failure_reason: "EXIT_CODE=129"` and a note saying it was invalidated (eval used wrong test set, checkpoint deleted). It was marked success only because its metrics were backfilled, but the log file was apparently never tracked.

| 0.0027 | -97.8% |\\\\n| proved_only ISR | 0.132 | 0.0028 | -97.9% |\\\\n| components | 6.58 | 18.55 | +182% |\\\\n| ISR=0 ratio | - | 94.8% | - |\\\\n| n_necessary=0 ratio | - | 94.8% | - |\\\\n\\\\n## Key findings:\\\\n1. PR improves (+3%) but ISR collapses near-zero (0.0027). The model gets BETTER at proving while becoming WORSE at faithful reasoning.\\\\n2. Component count nearly tripled (6.58→18.55). Model generates longer proofs with more components, but 94.8% have ZERO necessary components.\\\\n3. NOT tautological collapse (comp=2 ratio=0). Instead: contradiction-based collapse — model embeds P(a) and ¬P(a) among many irrelevant premises, making anything provable via explosion.\\\\n4. Windowed ISR trend is monotonically decreasing: 0.1115 (101-200) → 0.0433 (401-500) → 0.0200 (601-700) → 0.0144 (901-1000). No recovery signal.\\\\n5. The exec-only reward creates a pure reward hacking incentive: the model discovers that contradictions + trivially true conclusions always satisfy the prover.\\\\n\\\\n## Implication for paper:\\\\nExtended training is a clean ablation showing exec-only reward's failure mode worsens over time.
